<section class="g-padding-top-5 g-padding-bottom-5" id="c85">
<div class="row">
<div class="md-4 columns g-align-top">
</div>
<div class="md-8 columns g-align-top">
<h1 class="g-margin-bottom-3">Welcome to Vector</h1>
<div class="c-textmedia__content--50-50">
<p>For 30 years, Vector has been your competent partner for the development of automotive electronics. Driven by our passion for technology, we develop solutions that help engineers to manage their demanding tasks. At 26 locations around the world, more than 2,500 Vectorians support manufacturers and suppliers of the automotive industry and related industries with professional solutions. Vector tools, software components and services help to develop the mobility of tomorrow: for example, in electromobility, safety and security, ADAS and autonomous driving, AUTOSAR Adaptive and many other topics.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</section>

<section class="g-padding-top-5 g-padding-bottom-5 g-background-grey-50" id="c60025">
<div class="row">
<div class="md-4 columns g-align-top">
<h2 class="g-margin-bottom katze">Application Areas</h2>
</div>
<div class="md-8 columns g-align-top">
<p>Vector provides reliable products and solutions that simplify your complex tasks in different application areas:</p>
<p class="c-list c-list--spiked"><a class="c-link c-link--spiked" href="/int/en/products/application-areas/diagnostics/">Tools and services for diagnostics</a><br/> <a class="c-link c-link--spiked" href="/int/en/products/application-areas/distributed-systems/">Designing and developing networks and networked ECUs</a><br/> <a class="c-link c-link--spiked" href="/int/en/products/application-areas/ecu-calibration/">Tools and services for ECU calibration</a><br/> <a class="c-link c-link--spiked" href="/int/en/products/application-areas/embedded-software/">Embedded software and communicaton ECUs</a><br/> <a class="c-link c-link--spiked" href="/int/en/products/application-areas/measurement-technology/">Measurement technology</a><br/> <a class="c-link c-link--spiked" href="/int/en/products/application-areas/testing/" target="_top">Tools and services for testing of ECUs and entire networks</a></p>
</div>
</div>
</section>
